- **trellis-validate**: ODG validation gate A4 — demand evidence before the build is committed. Use when the user says "validate", "validation", "pre-order test", "landing page test", "do people want this", "fake door", "concierge", "design partner", or the router reaches A4 or needs a threshold derived for an A3 test. Researches benchmarks, computes the economic floor, presents them for the owner to set a threshold, pre-registers each test, generates the instruments, reports results against the threshold, and packages the build / pivot / stop decision for the owner. Role: audience (frontier). ([thinkinglemur/trellis](https://github.com/thinkinglemur/trellis/tree/HEAD/skills/trellis-validate))
- **trellis-verify-prd**: Audit prd.md against the original requirements doc (screenshots / tables / prose) via forward check (PRD→source) + reverse coverage scan (source→PRD); flag missing, misinterpreted, embellished, or UI-copy-mismatch items and propose edits. Triggers: 「校验 PRD」「对照原始需求」「verify PRD」. Not for PRD generation (trellis-brainstorm) or implementation-vs-PRD check (trellis-check-all). - **trellis2**: TRELLIS.2 — Microsoft's 4B-param image-to-3D model with O-Voxel representation. Produces PBR textured meshes (GLB) from single images. MIT license. SOTA for vehicles, mechanical objects, and complex topology. Supports Replicate API for cloud inference. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round24/TRELLIS2) · ★15)
- **trellis2**: Generate high-fidelity 3D meshes from text or images using Microsoft TRELLIS.2's native o-voxel representation. Use when you need production-quality game assets with optional PBR texturing in a single forward pass. The v2 architecture is more compact and faster than TRELLIS v1, with native texturing support. MIT licensed. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round6/trellis2) · ★15)
- **trellis2-lora**: Fine-tune the geometry of TRELLIS.2 (Microsoft's image-to-3D model) with LoRA on a user's own 3D meshes, and generate meshes from images with the resulting adapters. Runs on Hugging Face Jobs (or local Docker with an NVIDIA GPU) using the prebuilt `trellis2-prep` and `trellis2-train` images; data, checkpoints and metrics all live on the Hub, with Trackio dashboards. Use when the user mentions TRELLIS.2 or TRELLIS, image-to-3D fine-tuning, training a 3D generation model on a category of objects (shoes, chairs, furniture, products), LoRA for 3D geometry, preprocessing meshes into TRELLIS.2 late… ([suvadityamuk/trellis2-finetuning](https://github.com/suvadityamuk/trellis2-finetuning/tree/HEAD/skills/trellis2-lora) · ★10)
- **trellis3**: Generate production-grade 3D meshes from text or images using Microsoft TRELLIS.3's native o-voxel + structured latent hybrid representation. Use when you need AAA-quality game assets with native PBR texturing and structured part output in a single forward pass. Successor to TRELLIS v1 (CVPR 2025 Spotlight) and TRELLIS.2 (2026). 2026 release — adds multi-modal token fusion, improved PBR shader head, and Gaussian-splat fallback export. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round7/trellis3) · ★15)
- **trellis4**: Generate production-grade 3D meshes from text, images, or sparse views using Microsoft TRELLIS.4's full SLAT structured latent representation. Use when you need AAA-quality game assets with native PBR texturing, multi-view fusion, and explicit UV unwrap in a single forward pass. Successor to TRELLIS v1/v2/v3. 2026 release — adds multi-view fusion, built-in UV unwrap, and improved cascaded refinement. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round8/trellis4) · ★15)
- **trellis5**: Generate 3D meshes from text or images using TRELLIS v5 — Microsoft's 5th-generation structured latent diffusion model with native multi-modal encoder fusion, dual mesh+splat export, and built-in 8K PBR textures. Use when you need top-tier mesh fidelity with full PBR and the option to fall back to Gaussian splat rendering for hybrid pipelines. 2026 release — newest TRELLIS generation. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round10/trellis5) · ★15)
- **trellis6**: Generate 3D meshes from text or images using TRELLIS v6 — Microsoft's 6th-generation structured latent diffusion with sparse voxel SLAT, native multi-modal encoder fusion at the input layer, dedicated splat-native export (no dual-head needed), and 16K PBR textures. Use when you need top-tier mesh fidelity with a splat-first pipeline or the highest possible texture resolution. 2026 release — newest TRELLIS generation, building on TRELLIS v1-v5. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round11/trellis6) · ★15)
- **trellis7**: Generate 3D meshes from text or images using TRELLIS v7 — Microsoft's 7th-generation structured latent diffusion with native dual-head output (mesh + Gaussian splat simultaneously from same inference), 32K PBR textures, and multi-view diffusion fusion. Use when you need the absolute highest texture resolution with zero dual-head overhead, or when you need both mesh and splat output from the same inference. 2026 release — newest TRELLIS generation, building on TRELLIS v1-v6. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round12/trellis7) · ★15)
- **trellisx-add**: ➕ 规划级入口 (只规划不执行): 把指定请求纳入 trellis task 的 planning 阶段 —— 判新旧 + task.py create 登记 + 交互式 planning (brainstorm 主导 + grill 硬门1 边问边写), 产出 prd/design/implement 后停在 task.py start 之前, task 留 planning 态, 禁 exec/check/finish。用户想"先看规划再决定执行 / 添加分析规划任务 / 只规划不动手"时用。仅显式调用 (/trellisx-add), 禁 model 自动触发。与 trellisx-flow 边界: flow=强制全闭环 (plan→exec→check→finish), add=只到 planning 停; 执行 pending 规划态 task 走 /go ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-add) · ★5)
- **trellisx-apply**: 🛠️ 把 worktree隔离 + 闭环收尾 + task.md看板 三维注入当前项目 .trellis/。全部为 trellis 生命周期 hook 确定性强制 (after_start 建 worktree, after_finish 自动 commit→merge→archive→销 worktree, taskmd hook 维护看板), 不靠 AI 跑脚本。结果导向: 只约束最终行为闭环 (worktree 建/销 + create→…→finish 收尾链完整), 不约束注入方式。幂等 (marker 包裹) ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-apply) · ★5)
- **trellisx-cleanup**: 🧹 批量收尾 —— 一次清理/归档/收尾**全部**已完成 task。三件事: ① 清理所有已合并的 worktree ② 归档所有已完成的任务 ③ 清理 task.md 看板的已完成行。完成判定 = completed ∪ merged (并集), 当前 active task 永不纳入。**强制 dry-run → 用户确认 → --apply 执行** 三段护栏, worktree 销毁沿用安全判据 (脏/未合并自动保留, 不丢提交) ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-cleanup) · ★5)
- **trellisx-flow**: 🔄 请求级入口: 把指定请求强制纳入 trellis task 闭环 (plan→exec→check→finish), 禁 inline (即使极简)。用户显式调用 (/trellisx-flow) 或 model 自动触发 (请求复杂/多步/跨文件时)。调用即创建同意信号, 仍先判新建 vs 并入 active task。与 trellisx-apply 互补 —— apply 注入"推荐建 task"常驻软提示指向本 skill, 由本 skill 接管。载体默认 subagent 编排 (main 调度 trellis-implement 共享 task worktree 并发 2) ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-flow) · ★5)
- **trellisx-grill**: 🔥 对抗式审查 trellis 任务工件 (prd / design / implement / spec / subtask 文件), 贯穿 plan 前/中/后全程逐分支 stress-test 设计树, 帮用户确认/审查/拆解需求。逐问审 (可一次多问批量确认提效 + 推荐答案 + codebase 能答先查), 产物 = 工件内联批注 + 弱点表 (不改写工件, 由 orchestrate/spec 决定怎么改)。独立全周期可调, plan 前 / planning 中 / start 前 / spec 重构前 / 任意决策点都能用。源于 grill-me (relentless interview) + 项目盲点实证 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-grill) · ★5)
- **trellisx-orchestrate**: 🎯 在 Trellis 项目 planning 阶段, 指导编写 task 文件夹内的 prd.md / design.md / implement.md, 把任务编排理念 (五要素拆分、执行层选择、资源互斥、失败回退) 内嵌到文档中。让后续 dispatch 与执行有据可依, 而不是写完 PRD 才临时想怎么拆 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-orchestrate) · ★5)
- **trellisx-spec**: 📐 初始化 / 优化 / 重写 .trellis/spec/ 规则文档, 允许破坏式变更 (丢弃旧版本、合并、拆分、推翻原结构), 把描述性条款改为可机器验证的命令式契约 (MUST / 禁 / 严禁)。流程: 诊断 (初始化跳过) → 提案 → AskUserQuestion 强制审批 → 执行 + 同步 task manifest 引用清单。严禁未确认改写。sediment 模式 = finish 前判定门 (5 正向 + 3 排除 checklist, 有增量才沉淀, 全否跳过); planning 时 spec 加载归 trellisx-orchestrate step 1 grep 门 (本 skill 不负责加载, 仅提供 spec 内容) ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-spec) · ★5)
- **trellisx-workspace**: 📋 维护 `.trellis/task.md` 任务看板 —— trellis 缺的跨任务总览。**一个表格, 一行一个任务**, 列为 id/名称/描述/状态/worktree/前置 (6 列; 状态列承载生命周期阶段: 规划中/实施中/检查中/收尾/已完成/已归档; 前置列=该 task 依赖的前置 task ID, 承载 task 级 DAG, 仅有依赖者标, 无依赖填 —)。在 task create/start/阶段切换/archive/设依赖 后**及时更新**对应行; 并**自动清理超 7 天的已完成行**防膨胀。保持看板与 task.json 实时一致 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/tools/trellisx/skills/trellisx-workspace) · ★5)
